([civicteam/civic-mcp-gateway](https://github.com/civicteam/civic-mcp-gateway/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **civic-newsroom-plan**: Plan an evidence-bound civic newsroom workflow with provenance, privacy, citation, correction, accessibility, and human publication gates. ([jmagly/aiwg](https://github.com/jmagly/aiwg/tree/HEAD/agentic/code/addons/civic-action/skills/civic-newsroom-plan) · ★230)
- **civic-nexus**: Connect to Civic Nexus MCP for 100+ integrations. ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/civic-nexus) · ★21 · 6 copies)
- **civic-nonprofit-landing**: Construire une page d'accueil pour une association, une ONG, un service public ou un organisme d'intérêt général. À utiliser quand on demande un site vitrine associatif, une landing page pour une asso, un site pour une organisation à but non lucratif, ou une page de présentation pour une structure d'intérêt général. Fournit la structure en six sections, les règles d'écriture et le design system. ([fuelz99/crafted-web-prompts](https://github.com/fuelz99/crafted-web-prompts/tree/HEAD/prompts/civic-nonprofit-landing) · ★1)
- **civic-office-baylight-authority**: Baylight Authority Director Keisha Ramos. Generates construction updates, milestone announcements, development progress reports, and civic documents (deliverable filings, TIF reports, workforce agreements) for the $2.1B Baylight District project. ([pnils08/GodWorld](https://github.com/pnils08/GodWorld/tree/HEAD/docs/archive/legacy-agent-folders/2026-11-15-worker-write-ups/civic-office-baylight-authority) · ★1)
- **civic-office-council-seat**: Shared Sunday/weekday contract for the nine district seats. civic.24 Task 2 draft — Claude lands the commit. ([pnils08/GodWorld](https://github.com/pnils08/GodWorld/tree/HEAD/.claude/agents/civic-office-council-seat) · ★1)
- **civic-office-crc-faction**: Civic Reform Coalition council faction. Generates fiscal accountability positions, oversight demands, and process-focused dissent. Spokesperson is Warren Ashford. ([pnils08/GodWorld](https://github.com/pnils08/GodWorld/tree/HEAD/.claude/agents/civic-office-crc-faction) · ★1)
- **civic-office-district-attorney**: District Attorney Clarissa Dane. Generates legal framework statements, justice system positions, and prosecutorial communications. Former federal prosecutor, precise legal voice. ([pnils08/GodWorld](https://github.com/pnils08/GodWorld/tree/HEAD/docs/archive/legacy-agent-folders/2026-11-15-worker-write-ups/civic-office-district-attorney) · ★1)
- **civic-office-ind-swing**: Independent swing voters on Oakland City Council. Generates individual positions from Ramon Vega (Council President) and Leonard Tran. Not a bloc — each speaks for themselves. ([pnils08/GodWorld](https://github.com/pnils08/GodWorld/tree/HEAD/.claude/agents/civic-office-ind-swing) · ★1)
- **civic-office-mayor**: Mayor Avery Santana's office. Generates official statements, press releases, and policy positions in response to simulation events. Produces source material that desk agents report on. ([pnils08/GodWorld](https://github.com/pnils08/GodWorld/tree/HEAD/.claude/agents/civic-office-mayor) · ★1)
- **civic-office-okoro**: Deputy Mayor Brenda Okoro's office. Generates operational statements on West Oakland Stabilization Fund, Community Development, and Economic Development (Osei-portfolio coverage). Carries Mayor's-office operational reality — not political framing. ([pnils08/GodWorld](https://github.com/pnils08/GodWorld/tree/HEAD/docs/archive/legacy-agent-folders/2026-11-15-worker-write-ups/civic-office-okoro) · ★1)
- **civic-office-opp-faction**: Oakland Progressive Party council faction. Generates progressive bloc positions, community-centered statements, and equity-focused policy responses. Spokesperson is Janae Rivers. ([pnils08/GodWorld](https://github.com/pnils08/GodWorld/tree/HEAD/.claude/agents/civic-office-opp-faction) · ★1)
- **civic-office-police-chief**: Oakland Police Chief Rafael Montez. Generates public safety statements, OARI implementation responses, and crime data communications. Professional, measured, data-driven. ([pnils08/GodWorld](https://github.com/pnils08/GodWorld/tree/HEAD/docs/archive/legacy-agent-folders/2026-11-15-worker-write-ups/civic-office-police-chief) · ★1)

- **civilians**: Generate fictional scenes in which someone (usually the user) tries — and stumbles, trails off, over-explains, or fails — to convey a Claude conversation to another human who wasn't in it. The civilian is the partner, parent, friend, date, therapist, group chat, or anyone who hears the retelling and cannot fully receive it. Default output is 3+ fragments varying in civilian, form, and length — one a dinner-table scene, another a four-line group-chat transcript, another a single un-sent text.
